skirts are fitting a little looser even though I’m not trying to lose weight.\n- key: S027\n  name: Sleep_Disturbance\n  description: A state where there is a significant change in the quantity or pause before I can get my thoughts and words moving.\n- key: S029\n  name: Fatigue\n  description: Persistent fatigue that is not relieved by rest and a marked lack of energy.\n  manifestation: What Just getting through a normal workday and... and then managing dinner at home, it all just feels much heavier than it should right now.\n\nDoctor: Have you been feeling sad or down lately?\n\nPatient: Yes, I suppose I have. Most days lately, I just... I wake up with this heavy, gray feeling. It's almost like I'm already disappointed in the day before it even really starts, if that makes sense. It's just a very flat, down sort of mood that sticks with me.\n\nDoctor: Do you find yourself enjoying things you normally like to do?\n\nPatient: No, not really. I usually love painting, and... well, I normally enjoy wandering through museum exhibits, too. But recently, I just leave my art supplies sitting there untouched. I just can't seem to make myself care enough to actually go or do any of it right now.\n\nDoctor: Have you been feeling guilty about anything lately?\n\nPatient: Yes, I have. I just keep replaying things in my head... like every missed family plan, or every tense conversation I've had with my husband lately. It's hard not to feel like I'm just letting everyone down right now. I feel like I'm failing as a wife, as a mother, and even at work as a manager. I just feel guilty that I'm not doing enough for anyone.\n\nDoctor: Are you still able to find pleasure in activities that you normally enjoy, even if you're not motivated to do them?\n\nPatient: Like I mentioned a minute ago with my painting and the museum exhibits... it's really not just the motivation. I'm not locking myself away or ignoring phone calls from my friends. I still talk to people at work and see my family.
